TL;DR: HilDA是一个自监督预训练框架，通过层次化蒸馏（多层蒸馏+全局上下文蒸馏）和时序占用扩散目标，更好地将视觉基础模型的知识蒸馏到LiDAR主干网络中，在3D目标检测、场景流和语义占用预测等任务上达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法将VFMs视为黑盒教师，仅依赖逐帧特征相似性，未能充分利用教师网络的逐层语义结构、全局上下文以及LiDAR序列中丰富的时空信息。

Method: 提出HilDA框架，包含：(1) 层次化蒸馏——多层蒸馏实现渐进语义对齐 + 全局上下文蒸馏实现场景级语义；(2) 时序占用扩散目标促进时空一致性。

Result: 在跨模态蒸馏基准上达到SOTA，在3D目标检测、场景流和语义占用预测任务上优于先前蒸馏方法。

Conclusion: HilDA通过更充分地利用VFMs的层次语义和时空信息，有效提升了LiDAR主干网络的自监督预训练效果，为自动驾驶感知任务提供了更强的特征表示。

Abstract: Leveraging Vision Foundation Models (VFMs) for camera-to-LiDAR knowledge distillation offers a promising solution to the scarcity of annotated data needed to represent the immense geometric and kinematic diversity of real-world autonomous driving (AD). However, current approaches typically treat VFMs as black-box teachers, relying exclusively on frame-wise feature similarity. Consequently, they do not fully exploit the teacher's layer-wise semantic structure and global context, as well as the rich spatiotemporal information inherent in LiDAR sequences. We propose HilDA, a self-supervised pretraining framework for LiDAR backbones that better captures the semantic what and geometric where needed for driving tasks. HilDA combines hierarchical distillation comprising multi-layer distillation for progressive semantic alignment and global context distillation for scene-level semantics, with a temporal occupancy diffusion objective promoting spatiotemporal consistency. Models pre-trained with HilDA achieve state-of-the-art results on cross-modal distillation benchmarks and outperform models trained via prior distillation approaches on 3D object detection, scene flow, and semantic occupancy prediction. Code available at: https://maxiuw.github.io/hilda.

</details>


### [108] [Distill Once, Adapt Life-Long: Exploring Dataset Distillation for Continual Test-Time Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20196)
*Hyun-Kurl Jang,Jihun Kim,Hyeokjun Kweon,Kuk-Jin Yoon*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DO-ALL通过数据集蒸馏技术将源数据压缩为少量合成锚点，在源无关的在线测试时自适应中提供稳定参考，有效缓解长期分布偏移下的灾难性遗忘和自训练错误累积问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有源无关的连续测试时自适应方法在长期分布偏移下会因自训练错误累积和灾难性遗忘而变得不稳定，且实际部署中常因隐私或许可限制无法保留源数据集。

Method: 部署前对源数据进行数据集蒸馏，生成少量合成锚点以概括源分布；部署后每个目标样本与语义最对齐的锚点匹配，通过源重放、表示对齐和流形平滑正则化提供稳定参考。

Result: 在CIFAR100-C、ImageNet-C和CCC基准测试中，DO-ALL可无缝集成到现有CTTA算法中，持续提升长期鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 利用数据集蒸馏技术可以无需保留原始源数据即可实现稳定且持续的领域自适应，为源无关CTTA提供了一种有效的即插即用框架。

Abstract: Continual Test-Time Adaptation (CTTA) aims to maintain model performance under evolving target domains by adapting online without labeled data. However, practical deployments often cannot retain the source dataset due to privacy or licensing constraints, and purely source-free CTTA methods tend to become unstable under long-term distribution shift, suffering from compounding self-training errors and catastrophic forgetting. We introduce DO-ALL (Distill Once, Adapt Life-Long), a plug-and-play framework that revisits source information in a compact and privacy-conscious form via Dataset Distillation (DD). Before deployment, DO-ALL performs DD to produce a small set of synthetic distilled anchors that summarize the source distribution. During adaptation, each target sample is matched with its most semantically aligned anchor, which provides a stable reference for various CTTA via source replay, representation alignment, and manifold-smoothing regularization. DO-ALL can be seamlessly integrated into existing CTTA algorithms, consistently improving long-term robustness across CIFAR100-C, ImageNet-C, and the CCC benchmark. This demonstrates the potential of leveraging DD to enable stable and continuous adaptation without retaining raw source data. The code is available at https://github.com/blue-531/DOALL.

</details>


### [109] [Evaluation of Image Matching for Art Skills Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20199)
*Asaad Alghamdi,Michael Poor,Trung-Nghia Le,Tam V. Nguyen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出利用计算机视觉技术（SIFT特征和Siamese网络）通过匹配手绘图像与原始模板来评估绘画技能水平，实验表明基于SIFT的关键点匹配方法更为有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统绘画技能评估过程繁琐、耗时，缺乏客观量化标准，需要一种自动化、客观的方法来替代传统的人工评估。

Method: 采用计算机视觉技术，分别实现并分析了SIFT特征匹配和Siamese网络两种方法，通过计算手绘图像与原始模板之间的图像相似度来评估绘画技能。

Result: 实验结果表明该方法可行；通过特征分析发现，SIFT-based关键点匹配在检测绘画技能方面比Siamese网络更有效。

Conclusion: 利用计算机视觉进行绘画技能自动化评估是可行的，其中SIFT特征匹配方法效果更优，为绘画技能评估提供了客观量化手段。

Abstract: While some individuals possess a natural talent for drawing, mastering this skill requires dedicated training and practice. Determining one's skill in the art of drawing requires proper comprehensive assessment. In this paper, we propose a method to measure drawing skill by by matching the hand-drawn image with the original template. Existing techniques often involve complex processes. However, advancements in computer vision allow us to train computers to perform these comparisons at a human-like level, thereby resolving the tedious and overwhelming traditional process. Using computer vision applications, determining image similarity involves identifying the level of similarities in an image with a reference image. We have implemented and analyzed the SIFT feature and Siamese network to measure image similarity. Our results indicate that it is feasible to assess art skill levels. Through feature analysis, we found that SIFT-based key point matching provides a more effective means of detecting drawing skills.

</details>


### [110] [DeepForestVisionV2: Ecology-Driven Taxonomy Expansion for Camera-Trap Monitoring in African Tropical Forests](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20223)
*Hugo Magaldi,Theau d'Audiffret,Etienne Francois Akomo-Okoue,Bala Amarasekaran,Naomi Anderson,Claire Auger,Noemie Cappelle,Daniel Cornelis,Raphael Cornette,Tobias Deschner,Gabriel Dubus,Davy Fonteyn,Rosa M. Garriga,Jennifer Hatlauf,Innocent Kasekendi,Raymond Katumba,Aram Kazandjian,Alfred Ngomanda,Stephan Ntie,Simone Pika,Xavier Rufray,Harold Rugonge,John Justice Tibesigwa,Peter van Lunteren,Hadrien Vanthomme,Joeri A. Zwerts,Sabrina Krief*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DeepForestVisionV2 将原有35类分类空间扩展到64类（61种动物+人/车/空白），针对垂直分层、场景开阔度和人为界面三个部署梯度优化，在跨站点验证集上达到0.86准确率和0.82宏F1，在河岸、林缘等边缘场景显著提升了识别性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原有DeepForestVision仅针对封闭冠层林下地面设计，35类预测空间过于粗糙，无法有效处理树栖灵长类、鸟类、半水生动物及人畜等混杂场景。随着监测范围扩展到河岸、林缘等开放区域，亟需扩展分类体系以覆盖垂直分层、场景开阔度和人为界面三个部署梯度。

Method: 将分类从35类扩展至64类（61类动物+人/车/空白），使用来自多国非洲热带雨林项目的1,535,010张照片和243,354个视频进行训练，保留原有的离线工作流。评估采用跨站点裁剪照片验证集和三个针对部署梯度的乌干达视频基准。

Result: 验证集准确率0.86、宏F1 0.82、平衡准确率0.81；森林内部视频识别类群从22增至29，河岸从4增至9；公园边缘场景准确率从0.62提升至0.86，误报从11降至0。

Conclusion: DeepForestVisionV2在保持跨站点鲁棒性的同时，显著提升了野外实用性，能有效应对不同栖息地和相机陷阱设置下的识别挑战。

Abstract: Camera-trap monitoring in African tropical forests increasingly extends beyond closed-canopy interiors to riverbanks, clearings, and park edges. Among available open tools for African forest camera-trap classification, DeepForestVision is the only one providing a matched offline workflow for both photographs and videos, and previous work showed that it outperformed other available baselines on a comparable benchmark. However, it was designed for closed-canopy, ground-level forest interiors and uses a 35-class prediction space that becomes too coarse when deployments encounter arboreal primates, birds, semi-aquatic taxa, or human-associated confounders such as livestock. We present DeepForestVisionV2, an ecology-driven expansion from 35 to 64 prediction classes (61 animal classes plus human, vehicle, and blank) designed to address three recurrent deployment gradients: vertical stratification, scene openness, and anthropogenic interfaces. DeepForestVisionV2 retains the same offline workflow and is trained on 1,535,010 photographs and 243,354 videos from multi-country African tropical-forest projects. Evaluation combines a cross-country cropped-photo validation set, used to assess robustness across sites and camera-trap settings, with three held-out Uganda video benchmarks spanning the targeted gradients. On the validation set, DeepForestVisionV2 reaches 0.86 accuracy, 0.82 macro-F1, and 0.81 balanced accuracy. On the deployment benchmarks, it preserves or improves baseline accuracy despite its harder classification task, while increasing the number of identified taxa from 22 to 29 in forest-interior videos and from 4 to 9 at riverbanks. In the park-edge use case, it raises accuracy from 0.62 to 0.86 and reduces false alarms from 11 to 0. These results show that DeepForestVisionV2 materially improves field utility while preserving robustness across sites, habitats, and camera-trap settings.

</details>


### [111] [Cinematic Compositing Using Character-Environment-Harmonized Video Generation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20233)
*Tianyi Xiang,Mingming He,Li Ma,Jing Liao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种端到端视频扩散框架，通过三掩码引导的RGB-D联合去噪架构和先验驱动的数据策展流程，实现角色与环境的双向物理交互（C2E）和光照协调（E2C），在电影级动态视频合成中显著超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有电影级遮罩合成方法无法捕捉角色与环境之间复杂的双向交互，包括角色对环境（C2E）的物理交互和环境对角色（E2C）的光照协调，特别是交互道具带来的挑战。

Method: 1) 提出端到端视频扩散框架，联合建模C2E和E2C交互；2) 设计三掩码引导架构配合RGB-D联合去噪，确保角色、道具与环境间物理一致性；3) 开发先验驱动的数据策展流程，无需昂贵渲染即可构建高质量重光照配对数据；4) 引入参考条件机制实现可控环境合成和精确道具替换。

Result: 在电影级动态视频合成任务中显著优于现有方法。

Conclusion: 提出的双交互建模框架有效解决了电影级合成中角色-环境双向物理与光照交互问题，为高质量动态视频合成提供了新范式。

Abstract: Cinematic compositing aims to integrate green-screen characters into novel environments while maintaining physical and photometric realism. Previous methods often fail to capture the complex bidirectional interactions between characters and their surroundings, which we characterize as Character-to-Environment (C2E) physical interaction and Environment-to-Character (E2C) lighting harmonization. To address this, we propose an end-to-end video diffusion framework that jointly models C2E and E2C interactions, specifically handling the challenges of interactive props. Our approach introduces a tri-mask-guided architecture with RGB-D joint denoising to ensure physically consistent interactions among the character, props, and environment. We further develop an efficient prior-driven data curation pipeline to construct high-quality relighting pairs without expensive rendering. Finally, a reference-conditioned mechanism enables controllable environment synthesis and precise prop replacement. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our framework significantly outperforms existing methods in cinematic-quality dynamic video compositing.

</details>


### [112] [BAFIS: Dataset + Framework to assess occupational Bias and Human Preference in modern Text-to-image Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20241)
*Thomas Klassert,Adrian Ulges,Biying Fu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了五种主流文生图模型（Midjourney v6.1、Stable Diffusion 3 Medium、DALL-E 3、Playground v2.5和FLUX.1-dev）在职业图像生成中的性别和种族偏见、图像质量及提示对齐性，通过构建BAFIS平台收集人类反馈，并利用21,140张多语言提示生成的合成图像进行分析，发现模型存在系统性偏见，且现有自动评估指标与人类主观评分仅部分相关，强调了纳入人类偏好评估的必要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究表明图像生成模型受偏见影响严重，但缺乏对当前主流模型在职业图像生成中偏见的系统性评估，尤其缺少结合人类偏好反馈的综合分析。

Method: 构建了BAFIS（Battle-Arena for Fair Image Synthesis）平台收集人类偏好反馈；创建包含21,140张多语言提示生成的合成图像数据集；评估五种主流文生图模型的性别和种族偏见、图像质量及提示对齐性；将结果与德国联邦就业局官方统计数据对比。

Result: 发现所有模型均存在系统性性别和种族偏见；自动评估指标与人类主观评分仅部分相关；模型输出与德国就业统计存在偏差。

Conclusion: 开发更公平、更包容的文生图模型需要纳入人类偏好反馈，仅依赖自动指标不足以全面评估模型偏见。

Abstract: Generative artificial intelligence has the potential to improve productivity and transform the production of creative content. However, existing research indicates that image generation models are significantly influenced by biases. This work investigates the inherent biases and language-induced biases present in text-to-image models within the context of occupation-related image generation, complementing established metrics with human preference feedback. We present a comprehensive evaluation of five current text-to-image models: Midjourney v6.1, Stable Diffusion 3 Medium, DALL-E 3, Playground v2.5, and FLUX.1-dev , focusing on gender and ethnicity bias, image quality, and prompt alignment. To facilitate this evaluation, we developed the "Battle-Arena for Fair Image Synthesis" (BAFIS), a platform designed to collect human feedback on bias in generated images. Furthermore, we created a dataset comprising 21,140 synthetic images generated using multilingual prompts, which serves as a basis for our analysis. We further place our results within a broader social context by comparing them to official statistics from the German Federal Employment Agency. Our findings reveal systematic biases in text-to-image models, with established evaluation metrics in partial correlation with subjective user ratings. Thus, our research emphasizes the need for including human preferences to develop fairer and more inclusive text-to-image models.

</details>


### [113] [SPOT-E: Test-Time Entropy Shaping with Visual Spotlights for Frozen VLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20244)
*Bo Yin,Xiaobin Hu,Chengming Xu,Ruolin Shen,Mo Yang,Jiangning Zhang,Peng-Tao Jiang,Cheng Tan,Shuicheng YAN*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SPOT-E是一种即插即用的测试时方法，通过基于GRPO的轻量级调优生成问题条件化的视觉聚光灯，利用低熵锚点和熵整形目标解决视觉语言模型在证据密集型任务中因微小局部证据被忽视而导致的性能不佳问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉语言模型在证据密集型任务中表现不佳，因为决定性视觉证据往往微小、局部且容易被忽略，导致即使高层推理能力完好，证据读取环节仍会失败。现有推理时视觉干预方法缺乏验证高亮证据是否真正被模型使用的闭环机制。

Method: 提出低熵锚点和熵整形目标，以区分证据驱动的置信度与捷径塌陷导致的低熵。将上述原理实例化为SPOT-E方法：一种即插即用的测试时方法，基于GRPO进行轻量级每实例调优，生成问题条件化的视觉聚光灯。

Result: 在多个基准测试和不同VLM家族上，SPOT-E均取得一致性能提升，并在视觉损坏场景下表现出更强的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: SPOT-E通过熵整形有效区分了证据驱动与捷径塌陷的低熵状态，实现了无需重训练的视觉 grounding 增强，并验证了其在多种VLM架构下的通用性和鲁棒性。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) often underperform on evidence intensive tasks because decisive visual evidence are small, localized, and easy to overlook, leading to failures in evidence readout even when high-level reasoning is intact. Prior inference-time visual interventions can improve grounding without retraining, but they are largely open-loop and lack a mechanism to verify whether highlighted evidence is actually used. We study answer-span prediction entropy as a model-internal feedback signal and show that naive entropy minimization is ambiguous, since low entropy may arise from evidence-grounded confidence or shortcut collapse. To resolve this ambiguity, we introduce low-entropy anchors and an entropy-shaping objective that reduces answer uncertainty while preserving baseline high-confidence tokens. We instantiate this principle in SPOT-E, a plug-and-play test-time method that produces question-conditioned spotlights, optimized per instance via light-weight tuning based on Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO). Across all benchmarks and different VLM families, SPOT-E yields consistent gains and improved robustness under visual corruptions. Code is publicly available at: \url{https://github.com/YinBo0927/SPOT-E}

</details>


### [114] [Single-Stage Hierarchical Rectification for Weakly Supervised Histopathology Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20250)
*Duc T. Nguyen,Hoang-Long Nguyen,Thanh-Ha DO,Huy-Hieu Pham*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出单阶段层次化修正框架(SSHR)，通过层次化特征修正模块(HFRM)在前向传播中主动净化中间特征表示，直接在单训练循环内生成高保真激活图，无需多阶段CAM生成-伪掩膜精炼-全监督重训练范式，在LUAD-HistoSeg和BCSS数据集上超越现有SOTA方法，并减少2-5倍训练时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有计算病理学中的弱监督语义分割方法采用多阶段范式（CAM生成→离线伪掩膜精炼→全监督重训练），该解耦方法存在计算成本高、误差传播（浅层CNN的局部纹理偏差产生假阳性伪影且后续精炼难以修正）等根本性缺陷。

Method: 提出单阶段层次化修正(SSHR)框架，核心是层次化特征修正模块(HFRM)，利用深层全局语义上下文过滤浅层局部异常，在前向传播过程中主动净化中间特征表示，无需事后精炼，在单训练循环内直接生成高保真激活图。

Result: 在LUAD-HistoSeg和BCSS数据集上，SSHR优于现有SOTA多阶段方法，同时将训练时长减少2-5倍，降低了计算开销，加速了大规模组织病理学工作流的临床转化。

Conclusion: SSHR通过单阶段主动修正机制替代多阶段被动精炼范式，有效解决了误差传播和计算成本高的问题，为计算病理学中的弱监督语义分割提供了更高效、更准确的解决方案。

Abstract: Existing weakly supervised semantic segmentation (WSSS) methods in computational pathology rely on a multi-stage paradigm: class activation map (CAM) generation, offline pseudo-mask refinement, and fully supervised retraining. While established, this decoupled approach presents fundamental limitations. The multi-stage process not only incurs high computational training costs but also suffers from error propagation: local texture biases in shallow CNN layers generate false-positive artifacts that subsequent refinement steps often fail to correct. To address these persistent challenges through a simple yet highly effective approach, we propose the Single-Stage Hierarchical Rectification (SSHR) framework. Rather than passively refining CAMs post-hoc, our method proactively purifies intermediate feature representations during the forward pass. We introduce a Hierarchical Feature Rectification Module (HFRM) that utilizes deep global semantic context to filter out local anomalies in shallow layers. This mechanism generates high-fidelity activation maps directly within a single training loop. Experiments on the LUAD-HistoSeg and BCSS datasets demonstrate that SSHR outperforms state-of-the-art multi-stage methods. Furthermore, SSHR reduces training duration by 2 to 5 times. This efficiency minimizes computational overhead and accelerates clinical translation for large-scale histopathology workflows. The code is available at: https://github.com/trongduc-nguyen/SSHR

</details>


### [115] [U$^2$Mamba: A Two-level Nested U-structure Mamba for Salient Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20282)
*Junhui Li,Jialu Li,Youshan Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出U²Mamba，一种基于Mamba的U型网络用于显著性目标检测，通过多尺度Mamba U块(MMUBs)增强深度和局部特征提取，采用嵌套U结构整合不同感受野以获取更丰富的上下文信息，并提出层级训练监督方法替代传统深度监督方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于Mamba的显著性目标检测模型未能充分探索上下文信息和整个架构的深度，存在特征提取能力不足的问题。

Method: 1) 提出多尺度Mamba U块(MMUBs)增强模型深度以改善局部特征提取；2) 采用嵌套U结构整合浅层和深层的不同感受野，收集更丰富的上下文信息和更长距离数据；3) 提出层级训练监督方法，在训练过程中每层计算损失，替代传统的深度监督和顶层监督训练。

Result: 大量实验表明U²Mamba在显著性目标检测任务上取得了与最先进方法高度竞争的性能。

Conclusion: U²Mamba通过创新的网络结构和训练策略，有效提升了Mamba模型在显著性目标检测中的表现。

Abstract: Mamba-based models have emerged as a promising alternative for salient object detection (SOD), offering significant advantages in modeling long sequences. However, existing models often fail to explore contextual information and the depth of the entire architecture. This paper introduces U$^2$Mamba, a powerful and innovative U-structured network for salient object detection. We propose multiscale Mamba U-blocks (MMUBs) that enhance the model depth to improve local feature extraction capabilities. Our newly developed nested U-structure, incorporating MMUBs, enables the network to integrate various receptive fields from shallow and deep layers, thereby collecting richer contextual information and longer-range data without being constrained by resolution. Instead of using the traditional deep supervision scheme and top-level supervised training, we propose a hierarchical training supervision method where the loss is computed at each level during the training process. Extensive experiments demonstrate that U$^2$Mamba achieves highly competitive performance against state-of-the-art methods. The source code is available at \url{https://github.com/JL021/U2Mamba}.

</details>


### [116] [Geometry-Aware Superpixel Graph Transformer with Metadata for Skin Lesion Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20390)
*Muhammad Azeem,Tanveer Hussain,Amr Ahmed,Ardhendu Behera*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于区域的图学习框架，将皮肤镜图像中的病灶建模为超像素区域的图结构，通过边感知图 Transformer 和元数据上下文节点实现多模态融合，在四个公开数据集上超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有CNN/ViT方法通常依赖全局或补丁级特征，且将患者元数据通过后期融合方式简单结合，限制了空间层面的多模态推理能力。皮肤病变存在异质结构、强类内变异以及良恶性间细微差异，亟需一种能显式建模区域间几何关系并结构化整合临床信息的方法。

Method: 1) 将病灶分割为空间连贯的超像素区域，提取冻结CNN特征作为节点；2) 编码区域间几何关系作为边属性；3) 引入元数据上下文节点，连接所有区域节点，在相同关系空间中结构化整合人口统计学/临床变量；4) 使用边感知图Transformer更新节点表示，通过注意力驱动传播进行图级嵌入分类。

Result: 在四个公开基准数据集上的实验表明，显式的区域级关系建模和图原生多模态融合持续优于现有最先进方法。

Conclusion: 本文建立了新的图中心视角，将CNN特征建模为关系节点并通过上下文整合改进，实现了更具表达力和鲁棒性的分类，为皮肤癌诊断提供了新的范式。

Abstract: Automated skin cancer classification from dermoscopic images remains challenging due to heterogeneous lesion structure, strong intra-class variability, and subtle visual differences between benign and malignant cases. Existing CNN/ViT pipelines typically rely on global or patch-level features and often combine patient metadata via late fusion, which limits spatially grounded multimodal reasoning. We present a novel region-based graph learning framework that explicitly models lesions as graphs of spatially coherent superpixel regions represented as frozen CNN features. To capture fine-grained lesion arrangements, we encode inter-regional geometry as edge attributes and introduce a dedicated metadata context node connected to all regions, providing structured integration of demographic/clinical variables within the same relational space. Node representations are updated using our edge-aware graph transformer followed by attention-driven propagation, and a final graph-level embedding for benign-malignant classification. Experiments on four public benchmarks demonstrate that explicit region-level relational modeling and graph-native multimodal fusion yield consistent gains over the state-of-the-art. Consequently, we establish a new graph-centric perspective in which CNN features are modeled as relational nodes and improved through contextual integration, yielding more expressive and robust classifications.

</details>


### [117] [FlowBender: Feedback-Aware Training for Self-Correcting Conditional Flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20404)
*Daniel Gilo,Sven Elflein,Ido Sobol,Or Litany*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FlowBender 提出了一种闭框框架，通过在训练和推理过程中将条件扩散/流模型的与约束的偏差作为第一类输入，学习修正策略，从而同时提升生成样本的保真度和合理性，避免了传统监督方法和引导方法中二者的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有条件扩散和流模型在生成过程中常违反任务定义的约束（如深度条件模型生成的图像再提取深度与输入不符）。监督模型在推理时忽略对齐信息，引导方法通过手工设计的线性更新进行修正，但通常需要在条件保真度和样本合理性之间做权衡。其根本原因在于模型从未被训练去利用自身的对齐误差。

Method: FlowBender 是一个闭框框架，将推理时的对齐误差作为第一类输入。每一步包含：1) 未引导的前瞻 pass 估计干净信号；2) 通过前向算子计算任务特定偏差；3) 修正 pass 消费该偏差信号产生校正后的速度。对于可微算子使用梯度公式，不可微场景（如 JPEG 压缩）使用零阶变体。提出先验步骤捷径（prior-step shortcut）以最小额外计算成本实现闭框修正。

Result: 在图像到图像翻译、图像复原和3D网格纹理化等任务上，FlowBender 持续优于标准监督基线、对齐损失增强训练以及当前最先进的推理时引导方法，同时提升了保真度和合理性，而非在二者之间权衡。

Conclusion: FlowBender 通过将推理时的对齐误差显式引入训练过程，有效解决了条件生成模型中的约束违背问题，在不牺牲样本质量的前提下实现了更高的条件保真度，且适用于可微和不可微前向算子。

Abstract: Conditional diffusion and flow models routinely fail to satisfy the very constraints that define their task. For instance, a depth-conditioned model often produces images whose re-extracted depth disagrees with the input, even though the forward operator--the depth predictor defining the constraint--is available during both training and inference. Existing approaches generally fall into two categories: supervised models that treat the conditioning signal as a static cue and ignore alignment information at inference, and guidance-based methods that consult it through hand-tuned linear updates, typically trading fidelity to the condition against the plausibility of the generated sample. We argue that the fundamental gap in both paradigms is that the model is never trained to utilize its own alignment error. We introduce FlowBender, a closed-loop framework that treats this error as a first-class input, training the network to learn a correction policy conditioned on inference-time feedback. At each step, an unguided look-ahead pass estimates the clean signal, a task-specific deviation is computed via the forward operator, and a refinement pass consumes this signal to produce a corrected velocity. We propose several variants of FlowBender, including a gradient-based formulation for differentiable operators and a zero-order variant for non-differentiable settings such as JPEG compression. For efficient sampling, we introduce a prior-step shortcut that enables closed-loop correction at a minimal additional computational cost. Across image-to-image translation, restoration, and 3D mesh texturing, FlowBender consistently outperforms standard supervised baselines, alignment-loss-augmented training, and state-of-the-art inference-time guidance, improving fidelity and plausibility simultaneously rather than trading them against each other. Project page: https://flow-bender.github.io/

</details>


### [118] [Spectral Query-Key Product Weight Steering for Training-Free VLM Hallucination Mitigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20419)
*Karn Tiwari,Varnith Chordia,Prathosh A P*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出QK Product Steering方法，通过编辑注意力头中QK乘积的奇异模态来减少视觉语言模型的对象幻觉，无需数据、训练或额外推理成本，在GQA-based VLM上实现CHAIRs降低4.0%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉语言模型常生成流畅但与图像不符的描述（对象幻觉），现有方法多依赖于解码时干预或额外数据微调，缺乏轻量级、无成本的解决方案。

Method: 直接编辑每头QK乘积（预softmax注意力logits算子），通过抑制选定中间层少数主导奇异模态；将编辑后的乘积通过闭式查询仅更新映射回查询权重，保持共享键权重不变以兼容分组查询注意力；进一步将QK分解为对称和反对称分量以区分内容相似性与定向注意力模式。

Result: 在三种GQA-based VLM上平均相对CHAIRs降低4.0%，随机模态控制实验变化可忽略；消融实验表明幻觉信号集中于主导QK模态，且主要定位在对称互注意通道。

Conclusion: QK Product Steering提供了一种简单的解码时缓解替代方案，无需额外数据、微调或推理开销，同时基本保留通用多模态能力。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) often generate fluent but visually unsupported descriptions, especially by mentioning objects absent from the image. We propose QK Product Steering, a data-free, training-free, and zero-inference-cost weight edit for reducing object hallucination. The method directly edits the per-head query-key product, the operator that produces pre-softmax attention logits, by suppressing a small number of dominant singular modes in selected middle layers. The edited product is then mapped back to the query weights through a closed-form query-only update while keeping shared key weights fixed, making the edit compatible with grouped-query attention. We further decompose the QK product into symmetric and antisymmetric components to distinguish mutual content-similarity patterns from directional attention patterns. Across three GQA-based VLMs, QK Product Steering achieves an average relative CHAIR$_s$ reduction of $4.0\%$, while matched random-mode controls show negligible change. Interpretability ablations show that the hallucination signal is specific to dominant QK modes and is primarily localized to the symmetric mutual-attention channel. Overall, QK Product Steering offers a simple alternative to decoding-time mitigation, requiring no additional data, fine-tuning, or inference-time overhead while largely preserving general multimodal capability.

</details>


### [119] [InfantFace: Detecting infant faces in neonatal clinical environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20449)
*Abdullah Bin-Obaid,Maria M. Cobo,Rebeccah Slater,Lionel Tarassenko,Mauricio Villarroel*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于YOLOv11m的婴儿面部检测模型，在多个公开数据集上训练并在新生儿临床数据集上微调，最终AP50达到0.96，显著优于三种现有通用面部检测器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 新生儿临床环境中存在背景杂乱、光照变化、面部遮挡等挑战，导致现有面部检测模型准确率下降，而可靠的新生儿面部定位是后续疼痛表情分析、心肺信号提取等非接触评估的第一步。

Method: 采用YOLOv11m单阶段检测器，先在VGGFace2、CelebA、FDDB、WIDER FACE等多个公开数据集上训练，再在包含228个视频、114个记录会话、113名独立婴儿的新生儿研究数据集上进行微调（临床域适应）。

Result: 微调前AP50为0.87，超越三种最先进通用面部检测器；临床域适应后AP50提升至0.96。

Conclusion: 该方法有效提升了新生儿临床环境中的面部检测性能，但缺乏公开新生儿数据集仍是领域发展的主要障碍，未来需在隐私保护和伦理规范下创建此类数据集。

Abstract: Reliable localisation of the neonatal face is the first step for several video-camera based non-contact assessments such as pain and distress related facial expression analysis, pain scoring, cardiorespiratory signal extraction and cessation of breathing alerts. However, major challenges persist in neonatal clinical environments. Cluttered backgrounds, illumination changes and poor lighting conditions can reduce the accuracy of face detection models. Clinical interventions, monitoring equipment and, in some cases, medical devices can obstruct the face, making visual assessment difficult. We propose a one-stage YOLOv11m-based model tailored for face detection of infants in neonatal clinical environments. We combined multiple publicly available datasets (VGGFace2, CelebA, FDDB, WIDER FACE) to train and evaluate our proposed model. We then fine-tuned our model on a neonatal research dataset involving 228 videos from 114 recording sessions of 113 independent infants. Before fine-tuning, our model achieved an AP50 of 0.87, surpassing the performance of three state-of-the-art general face detectors. Performance improved further to an AP50 of 0.96 after clinical-domain adaptation. Evaluating face detection performance across different datasets remains a challenge due to the lack of publicly available neonatal datasets. Prioritising the creation of such datasets, while upholding appropriate privacy safeguards and ethical standards in their creation and use, would greatly support further progress in this field.

</details>


### [120] [PCFootprint: A Large-Scale Dataset and Benchmark for Vectorized Building Footprint Extraction from Aerial LiDAR Point Clouds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20455)
*Haoyuan Shen,Kuihao Wang,Ruisheng Wang,Yujun Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了PCFootprint，首个大规模公开的机载激光扫描点云建筑足迹提取数据集，包含33000个瓦片，覆盖爱沙尼亚多样城乡景观，并建立了主流方法的基准测试，揭示了高类内方差、数据不平衡和噪声等挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于光学影像的建筑足迹提取方法易受遮挡、透视畸变和残余地形位移影响，导致提取不完整或对齐不准，且缺乏显式高程信息，限制了在细节层级建筑建模中的直接应用。因此需要从机载激光扫描点云中提取建筑足迹的数据集。

Method: 构建了包含33000个瓦片（每个128×128米）的大规模数据集，源自爱沙尼亚土地与空间发展委员会，包含系统性对齐的矢量化足迹，并设立了3000个瓦片的跨域测试集以评估泛化能力。

Result: 通过评估主流方法建立了全面基准，实验表明在复杂地理环境中存在高类内方差、数据不平衡和噪声等显著挑战。

Conclusion: PCFootprint将推动建筑建模、城市场景理解和地理空间分析的未来研究。

Abstract: Building footprint extraction is a fundamental task in photogrammetry, remote sensing, and computer vision. Recent image-based methods have achieved remarkable progress in extracting vectorized footprints from high-resolution optical imagery. However, optical imagery inherently susceptible to occlusions, perspective distortions, and residual relief displacement, yielding incomplete or misaligned footprint extraction. Furthermore, the lack of explicit elevation information limits its direct applicability to Level of Detail building modeling. In this paper, we present PCFootprint, the first large-scale public dataset for footprint extraction from airborne laser scanning point clouds. PCFootprint comprises \num{33000} tiles derived from the Estonian Land and Spatial Development Board, covering diverse urban and rural landscapes. Each tile spans \qtyproduct{128 x 128}{\m} with systematically aligned vectorized footprints aligned to point clouds. The dataset includes a \num{3000} tiles cross-domain test set for evaluating generalization across geographic regions. We establish comprehensive benchmarks by evaluating mainstream methods. Experimental results reveal significant challenges including high intra-class variance, data imbalance, and noise across complex geospatial environments. We believe PCFootprint will advance future research in building modeling, urban scene understanding, and geospatial analysis. The PCFootprint dataset is publicly available at \url{https://huggingface.co/datasets/Haoyuan-Shen/PCFootprint}.

</details>


### [121] [Scalable Training of Spatially Grounded 2D Vision-Language Models for Radiology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20477)
*Yusuf Salcan,Simon Ging,Robin Schirrmeister,Philipp Arnold,Elmar Kotter,Behzad Bozorgtabar,Thomas Brox*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了RefRad2D，一个大规模双语（德/英）CT/MR影像-文本数据集，并训练了RadGrounder模型，该模型能同时进行报告生成、VQA和空间定位，且定位监督不会降低语言质量，展示了临床数据的可迁移性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有放射学视觉语言模型缺乏空间定位能力，且需要大量人工标注，本文旨在无需人工空间标注的条件下训练具备空间定位能力的VLM。

Method: 构建RefRad2D双语数据集（120万CT/MR影像-文本对），通过LLM自动策展和自动分割生成任务特定的VQA和空间定位子集；训练RadGrounder模型联合进行报告生成、VQA和边界框检测/分割式空间定位。

Result: 在Slake和VQA-RAD外部基准上取得与专用医学VLM竞争的结果；加入临床数据比仅在下游数据集微调效果更好；空间定位监督不损害语言生成质量。

Conclusion: 大规模自动标注的临床数据结合多任务训练可以有效赋予VLM空间定位能力，且不会影响文本生成性能，实现了可空间验证的输出。

Abstract: We study how to train visually grounded vision-language models (VLMs) for radiology without manual spatial annotations. We introduce RefRad2D, a large-scale bilingual (German/English) dataset of 1.2M CT and MR image-text pairs derived from clinical practice, with task-specific VQA and spatial grounding subsets generated automatically via LLM-based curation and automated segmentation. Trained on this data, our model RadGrounder jointly performs report generation, visual question answering, and spatial grounding via bounding-box detection or segmentation. On external VQA benchmarks (Slake, VQA-RAD), RadGrounder achieves competitive results with specialized medical VLMs. Adding our clinical data to the training mixture improves open-ended VQA over fine-tuning on the downstream datasets alone, showing the transferability of our dataset. Crucially, adding grounding supervision does not degrade language quality, enabling spatially verifiable outputs at no cost to VQA performance.

</details>


### [122] [How Fragile Are Training-Free AI-Generated Image Detectors? A Controlled Audit of Score Direction, Preprocessing, and Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20488)
*Jingwen Zhou,Mingzhe Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文对两种无训练AI图像检测器（基于自编码器重建分数和基于噪声扰动特征相似度分数）在统一协议下进行审计，发现三个警告性发现：实现细节伪装成方法差异、分数方向取决于超参数而非方法本身、数据集格式偏差膨胀了鲁棒性声称。审计表明这些分数具有互补的逐生成器失败集，但简单融合无法超越最佳单一分数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无训练AI图像检测器的性能报告缺乏统一受控协议下的比较，导致无法公正评估其真实能力。

Method: 在统一1500张图像的GenImage基准上，审计两种代表性无训练分数（AEROBLADE式重建分数和RIGID式噪声扰动特征相似度分数）及朴素特征kNN控制，涵盖7个生成器和JPEG压缩（质量70和50）条件。

Result: (i) 实现细节（骨干网络、预处理）显著改变性能；(ii) RIGID式分数方向随噪声水平翻转；(iii) 数据集格式偏差导致虚假鲁棒性。分数互补但简单z-score融合不优于最佳单一分数。

Conclusion: 无训练检测器的性能对实现细节和超参数高度敏感，需建立标准化评估协议；分数的互补性需要方向感知的融合策略才能有效利用。

Abstract: Training-free detectors of AI-generated images promise generator-agnostic deployment without classifier training, yet their reported numbers are rarely compared under a single controlled protocol. We audit two representative training-free scores -- an autoencoder-reconstruction score (AEROBLADE-style) and a noise-perturbation feature-similarity score (RIGID-style) -- plus a naive feature-kNN control, on a common 1,500-image GenImage-derived benchmark spanning seven generators and JPEG compression at quality 70 and 50. The audit yields three cautionary findings. (i) Implementation details masquerade as method differences: replacing the LPIPS backbone (AlexNet -> VGG-16) changes overall AUROC by +0.085, and switching between resize-to-512 and native-resolution preprocessing flips per-generator conclusions by up to 0.38 AUROC. (ii) Score direction is not a property of the method but of its hyperparameters: the RIGID-style score is inverted (AUROC < 0.5) on SD1.5 and Wukong at noise level sigma=0.05, recovers to >0.5 for every generator at sigma=0.01, and collapses to 0.15 at sigma=0.3. (iii) Dataset format bias inflates robustness claims: without unified re-encoding, AUROC under JPEG-50 exceeds the clean condition for the AlexNet-backbone reconstruction score; after bias correction the residual anomaly localizes to a single generator (BigGAN). The audited scores have complementary per-generator failure sets, but naive z-score fusion does not beat the best single score, indicating that exploiting complementarity requires direction-aware combination.

</details>


### [123] [FreeStyle: Free Control of Style-Content Dual-Reference Generation from Community LoRA Mining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20506)
*Jinghong Lan,Wei Cheng,Yunuo Chen,Ziqi Ye,Peng Xing,Yixiao Fang,Rui Wang,Yufeng Yang,Xuanyang Zhang,Xianfang Zeng,Difan Zou,Gang Yu,Chi Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出FreeStyle，一种基于社区LoRA挖掘的可扩展双参考图像生成框架，通过两阶段课程学习（注意力级内容富集约束+频率感知RoPE调制）解决风格与内容参考中的语义泄漏问题，并构建了大规模三元组数据集及包含内容对齐分数和拒绝分数的评估基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双参考生成（风格-内容）面临三大挑战：内容保真度、风格对齐与指令遵循之间的平衡难以兼顾，特别是风格参考中的语义泄漏问题；同时缺乏大规模、干净的内容-风格分离的三元组训练数据，限制了模型对长尾风格的覆盖能力。

Method: 1) 社区LoRA挖掘：将社区LoRA作为风格和内容的组合锚点，构建大规模风格-内容和内容-参考三元组；2) 两阶段课程学习：风格迁移阶段采用注意力级富集约束抑制风格参考泄漏，双参考阶段采用频率感知RoPE调制策略应对位置对应性泄漏；3) 风格不变内容对齐分数（CAS）和基于VLM的校准拒绝分数用于评估泄漏抑制。

Result: 实验表明FreeStyle在风格对齐、内容保真度和泄漏抑制之间实现了强平衡，在风格相似性、内容保持、美学质量、指令遵循和泄漏拒绝等多个维度均表现出色。

Conclusion: FreeStyle通过社区LoRA挖掘解决了双参考生成中数据稀缺问题，两阶段课程学习有效抑制了不同层面的语义泄漏，为风格-内容双参考生成提供了一种可扩展且效果优异的解决方案。

Abstract: Style-content dual-reference generation aims to synthesize an image that preserves the structure and semantics of a content reference while adopting the style of a separate style reference.Despite recent progress, this setting remains challenging because models must balance content fidelity, style alignment, and instruction following avoiding semantic leakage from the style reference.A key bottleneck is the lack of large-scale triplet data with clean content-style separation and broad long-tail style coverage.In this work, we propose FreeStyle, a scalable dual-reference generation framework based on community LoRA mining.We treat community LoRAs as compositional anchors for style and content, and design a rigorous generation and filtering pipeline to construct large-scale Style-Reference and Content-Reference triplets across multiple base models.To address content leakage, we adopt a two-stage curriculum with stage-specific disentanglement mechanisms: an attention-level enrichment constraint that suppresses style-reference leakage in the style-transfer stage, and a frequency-aware RoPE modulation strategy that targets positional-correspondence-based leakage in the harder dual-reference stage.We also introduce a benchmark covering both style-reference and dual-reference generation, with evaluations on style similarity, content preservation, aesthetics, instruction following, and leakage rejection. The benchmark incorporates a style-invariant Content Alignment Score (CAS) and introduces a calibrated VLM-based Rejection Score for evaluating generation reliability and leakage suppression.Extensive experiments show that our model achieves a strong balance among style alignment, content preservation, and leakage suppression.

</details>


### [124] [S-Agent: Spatial Tool-Use Elicits Reasoning for Spatial Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20515)
*Yalun Dai,Hao Li,Shulin Tian,Runmao Yao,Yuhao Dong,Fangzhou Hong,Zhaoxi Chen,Fangfu Liu,Baoliang Tian,Dingwen Zhang,Tao Wang,Kim-Hui Yap,Ziwei Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: S-Agent 是一种空间工具使用智能体范式，通过将空间推理建模为时空证据累积而非孤立的帧级预测，结合层次化空间工具和时序记忆机制，在无需训练的情况下提升多视图和视频空间推理性能，并可通过SFT训练出超越同类规模基线的紧凑空间智能体 S-Agent-8B。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLM和工具增强智能体主要局限于静态、无状态的单帧视觉观察推理，无法处理连续演进的3D世界，缺乏对时空连续性的空间智能。

Method: 将VLM作为语义规划器决定所需证据，通过层次化空间工具（2D定位→3D几何证据→高层空间知识）进行接地与聚合，并引入时序记忆机制（Scene Memory维护场景状态、Agent Memory累积推理上下文），实现跨帧和推理步骤的证据整合。

Result: 在多视图和视频空间推理基准上，S-Agent以无需训练的方式一致提升开源和闭源VLM；SFT后的S-Agent-8B显著超越同类规模基线（如Qwen3-VL-8B），性能媲美GPT-5.4和Gemini 3等先进闭源模型。

Conclusion: 将空间推理从帧级识别重构为场景中心理解，通过时空证据累积和工具层次化调用，能有效增强VLM的连续3D空间智能，且训练后可实现紧凑高效的专用空间智能体。

Abstract: Real-world spatial intelligence requires reasoning over a continuous and evolving 3D world, yet existing VLMs and tool-augmented agents largely remain tied to static, stateless inference from isolated visual observations. We introduce \textbf{\textsc{S-Agent}}, a spatial tool-use agentic paradigm for understanding and reasoning over continuous multi-view images and videos. By formulating spatial reasoning as spatio-temporal evidence accumulation rather than isolated frame-level prediction, \textsc{S-Agent} reshapes spatial perception into scene-centric understanding beyond frame-centric recognition. Specifically, \textsc{S-Agent} casts the VLM as a semantic planner that decides what evidence is needed, while a hierarchy of spatial tools and experts grounds objects in 2D, lifts them into 3D geometric evidence, and aggregates this evidence into high-level spatial knowledge (\textit{e.g.}, counting, measurement, orientation, and relative position). Additionally, a temporal memory mechanism, including Scene Memory for maintaining the evolving scene state and Agent Memory for accumulating reasoning context, enables evidence integration across frames and reasoning steps. Comprehensive experiments on multi-view and video spatial reasoning benchmarks show that \textsc{S-Agent} consistently improves both open-source and closed-source VLMs in a training-free manner. Beyond inference-time augmentation, supervised fine-tuning (SFT) on \textsc{S-Agent}-generated spatial trajectories \textsc{S-300K} yields \textsc{S-Agent-8B}, a compact spatial agent that significantly surpasses similar-scale baselines (e.g., Qwen3-VL-8B) and performs comparably to advanced closed-source models (e.g., GPT-5.4 and Gemini 3).

</details>


### [125] [HumanScale: Egocentric Human Video Can Outperform Real-Robot Data for Embodied Pretraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20521)
*Juncheng Ma,Jianxin Bi,Yufan Deng,Xuanran Zhai,Kewei Zhang,Ye Huang,Bo Liang,Shukai Gong,Jiankai Tu,Xiaotian Tang,Jiaxin Li,Kaiqi Chen,Duomin Wang,Yuqi Wang,Bingyi Kang,Eric Huang,Zhiyang Dou,Zhen Dong,Enze Xie,Wojciech Matusik,Tat-Seng Chua,Daquan Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文系统比较了以自我为中心的人类视频和遥操作真实机器人轨迹作为具身基础模型预训练数据源的效果，发现经过精心设计的过滤和标注流程处理后，自我中心数据不仅在模型预训练中是可替代的，而且能带来更优性能：在真实机器人动作预测上验证损失降低24%，分布内和分布外任务成功率分别提升52.5%和90%。这验证了一种可扩展的具身基础模型范式：先用自我中心人类视频预训练学习多样化的世界表征，再用少量标注的真实机器人数据进行动作空间对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 遥操作真实机器人轨迹作为预训练数据源存在采集成本高、获取难度大、行为和环境多样性低等瓶颈，而自我中心人类视频虽成本低且多样性强，但其与遥操作数据相比的有效性尚未被充分探索。本文旨在系统比较这两种数据源在具身基础模型预训练中的效果。

Method: 在固定后训练和验证协议下，系统比较自我中心人类视频和遥操作真实机器人轨迹作为预训练数据源。对自我中心数据采用精心设计的过滤和标注流程处理，然后在相同预训练数据量下对比模型在真实机器人动作预测和任务执行上的表现。

Result: 自我中心数据预训练的模型在真实机器人动作预测上验证损失降低24%，分布内任务成功率提升52.5%，分布外任务成功率提升90%。

Conclusion: 验证了可扩展的具身基础模型训练范式：先用自我中心人类视频预训练学习多样化世界表征，再用少量真实机器人数据微调实现动作空间对齐。建议在昂贵机器人数据收集前先进行数据质量评估，并鼓励更广泛探索自我中心数据。

Abstract: Embodied foundation models are expected to benefit from data scaling like large language models, but face a much tighter data bottleneck. Teleoperated real-robot trajectories remain the dominant pretraining source due to their precise action supervision and embodiment alignment, yet their scalability is limited by high collection cost, acquisition difficulty, and low behavioral and environmental diversity. These limitations have sparked interest in egocentric human video as a scalable, substantially lower-cost, and more diverse alternative for embodied model pretraining. However, its effectiveness compared to teleoperated real-robot data remains underexplored. To address this question, we conduct a systematic study comparing egocentric human video and teleoperated real-robot trajectories as pretraining data sources for embodied foundation models, under fixed post-training and validation protocols. Surprisingly, we find that egocentric data, when processed through a carefully designed filtering and labeling pipeline, is not merely a viable substitute for model pretraining but can lead to superior performance. With the same amount of pretraining data, models pretrained on egocentric data achieve a 24% lower validation loss on real-robot action prediction, as well as 52.5% and 90% higher success rates on in-distribution and out-of-distribution real-robot task execution, respectively. This finding verifies a scalable paradigm for embodied foundation models: pretrain on egocentric human video to learn diverse world representations, then adapt with a small amount of labeled real-robot data for action-space alignment. We hope this study encourages broader exploration of egocentric data and offers guidance for data quality assessment before costly robot data collection.

</details>


### [126] [SARLO-80: Worldwide Slant SAR Language Optic Dataset 80cm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20523)
*Solène Debuysère,Nicolas Trouvé,Nathan Letheule,Elise Colin,Georgia Channing*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个大规模甚高分辨率SAR-光学-文本多模态数据集SARLO-80，包含119,566个三元组（复值和幅度SAR斜距图像、对齐的光学图像、自然语言描述），覆盖72个国家257个地点，并公开了预处理代码和基准测试方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有SAR-光学数据集多基于低分辨率、仅强度的GRD产品，缺乏复值SAR测量和原生采集几何信息，限制了基于物理的多模态学习；且缺少结合甚高分辨率SAR SLC、对齐光学图像和自然语言描述的大规模公开数据集。

Method: 基于Umbra聚束模式SICD数据（约2500景，VV/HH极化，20cm-2m原生分辨率），将SAR数据标准化至80cm斜距网格（带限FFT重采样），分块为1024×1024切片；为每个SAR切片检索高分辨率光学图并通过局部坐标对应进行像素级对齐；生成三种长度（短/中/长）描述文本。

Result: 构建了119,566个三元组数据集，覆盖72个国家257个地点，包含多种土地类型和基础设施；提供固定的训练/验证/测试划分及完整预处理和基线代码。

Conclusion: 该数据集填补了VHR SAR SLC与光学、文本对齐的多模态数据集空白，支持跨模态检索和条件生成等任务的基准测试，已在Hugging Face公开。

Abstract: Multimodal foundation models have advanced rapidly thanks to large optical benchmarks, but comparable resources for synthetic aperture radar (SAR) remain limited. Existing SAR--optical datasets largely rely on low-resolution, intensity-only Ground Range Detected~(GRD) products and do not preserve complex-valued SAR measurements or native acquisition geometry, which restricts physically grounded multimodal learning. In particular, large-scale public datasets combining very-high-resolution (VHR) SAR SLC, aligned optical imagery, and natural-language descriptions are still lacking. We present a VHR SAR--optical--text dataset built from open-access Umbra spotlight acquisitions distributed as Sensor Independent Complex Data (SICD). From around 2,500 worldwide scenes (VV/HH, 20cm--2m native resolution), we standardize all SAR data to an 80cm slant-range grid via band-limited FFT resampling and tile the imagery into 1024 by 1024 patches. For each SAR patch, we retrieve a high-resolution optical tile and warp it into the SAR grid using local coordinate correspondences for local pixel-level alignment. We further generate three caption variants (SHORT/MID/LONG) per sample to support vision--language training and evaluation. Our dataset contains 119,566 triplets (complex and amplitude slant-range SAR patch, aligned optical patch, natural-language description) covering 257 locations across 72 countries and a broad range of land types and infrastructures. We release fixed train/validation/test splits and the full preprocessing and baseline code to enable reproducible benchmarks for multimodal alignment on cross-modal retrieval and conditional generation in native SAR geometry. The dataset is publicly available on the Hugging Face Hub at https://huggingface.co/datasets/ONERA/SARLO-80.

</details>


### [127] [VisDom: Sparse Novel View Synthesis with Visible Domain Constraint](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20531)
*Mariia Gladkova*,Tarun Yenamandra*,Edmond Boyer,Robert Maier,Tony Tung,Daniel Cremers*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VisDom是一种无学习的几何约束方法，通过定义至少被K个视图观测的可见域，在标准视觉壳重建基础上增加多视图可见性过滤，从而在稀疏视角新视图合成中提供更强的空间先验，有效减少伪影和不一致几何，适用于NeRF和3D GS管线，仅需轮廓掩码，无需可学习参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏视角下的新视图合成由于从少量输入视角恢复3D几何存在歧义性而具有挑战性。现有NeRF和3D GS方法在密集监督下表现良好，但在稀疏设置中容易过拟合，产生漂浮伪影和不一致的几何。虽然轮廓一致性常被用作正则化项，但仅靠轮廓约束仍不足以定位真实物体几何，因为轮廓一致区域可能超出物体真实范围。

Method: VisDom定义可见域为3D空间中至少被K个视图观测到的子集，并将其作为标准轮廓重建基础上的额外过滤准则。具体实现中，分别集成到隐式NeRF管线（限制体采样范围）和显式3D GS管线（引导高斯点放置位置），通过多视图可见性计数提供更强的空间先验。

Result: 在三个挑战性数据集上的实验表明，VisDom在稀疏视角NVS任务中取得一致改进，仅需4张输入图像即可实现高质量物体中心重建。将VisDom应用于GaussianObject可进一步提升Omni3D和MipNeRF360上的性能，同时以22倍更低的训练成本达到或超越原有水平。

Conclusion: VisDom是一种域无关、仅需轮廓掩码、无学习参数的简单几何约束方法，可轻松集成到现有NeRF和GS管线中，有效解决稀疏视角下的几何歧义问题，是现有方法的轻量级补充。

Abstract: Sparse novel view synthesis (NVS) remains challenging due to the ambiguity of recovering 3D geometry from few input views. While NeRF- and Gaussian Splatting (GS)-based methods perform well with dense supervision, they often overfit in sparse settings, producing floating artifacts and inconsistent geometry. Silhouette consistency is commonly used as a regularizer, but it remains insufficient, as silhouette-consistent regions can extend beyond the true object geometry. We introduce VisDom, a learning-free geometric constraint that augments classical carving-based visual hull reconstruction by enforcing a minimum multi-view visibility requirement. Specifically, we define a visible domain as the subset of 3D space observed by at least $K$ views and use it as an additional filtering criterion on top of standard silhouette-based reconstruction. This provides a stronger spatial prior in sparse-view settings. We integrate VisDom into both implicit (NeRF) and explicit (GS) pipelines by restricting volumetric sampling and guiding Gaussian placement during optimization. Experiments on three challenging datasets show consistent improvements in sparse-view NVS, enabling high-quality object-centric reconstruction from as few as four input images. Our method is domain-agnostic, requires only silhouettes, and introduces no learned parameters, making it a simple complement to existing approaches. Applying VisDom on top of GaussianObject further improves performance on Omni3D and MipNeRF360, while matching or surpassing it at 22 $\times$ lower training cost.

</details>


### [128] [The FID Lottery: Quantifying Hidden Randomness in Generative-Model Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20536)
*Nicolas Dufour,Alexei A. Efros,Patrick Pérez*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了FID作为图像生成评估指标的重复性问题，发现训练种子（重训练）引起的FID波动比采样种子（重采样）大3.2倍，并提出了新的评估协议：使用per-cell最优引导、将低于1.3%变异系数的FID差异视为不显著、报告多个训练种子的误差线而非单一数值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前大多数论文仅报告单个模型单次采样下的单一FID数值，但FID作为随机变量的重复性未知。作者旨在量化FID在训练和生成两个维度上的方差，揭示其在实践中的可靠性问题。

Method: 在数百个SiT网络上进行系统性实验，将FID视为训练种子和生成种子的二维随机变量，直接测量其方差，并分析随机初始化、数据顺序、流匹配损失中的高斯噪声等因素的影响。

Result: (a) 重训练引起的FID波动是重采样的3.2倍；(b) 该差距由随机初始化、数据顺序和流匹配噪声三种因素驱动；(c) 增加计算量或模型规模几乎不缩小FID散布（CoV维持在1-2%）；(d) per-cell CFG调优可减半散布但会改变最优种子的排序，幸运训练种子可在2倍更少计算量下达到相同FID。

Conclusion: 建议新的FID评估协议：在per-cell最优引导下评估，将低于~1.3% CoV的FID差距视为不确定，报告多个训练种子的误差线而非单一FID值。

Abstract: The Frechet Inception Distance (FID) is the de facto arbiter of image generation, yet most papers report just a single number from a single trained model using a single sampling seed. How reproducible is that number if we retrain the model, or merely resample from it? In this paper, we treat FID as a random variable on a two-axis panel of training and generation seeds, and measure its variance directly on several hundred SiT networks trained on class-conditional ImageNet 256x256. We report surprising findings: (a) Retraining the model using the same recipe with a different seed moves FID 3.2x more (in Inception feature space) than redrawing samples from a fixed network. (b) That gap is driven by three factors: random initialisation, data ordering, and the per-step Gaussian noise of the flow-matching loss. (c) Increasing compute or model size barely tightens the spread, holding the FID coefficient of variation (CoV) inside a 1-2% band. (d) Per-cell classifier-free-guidance tuning halves the spread but reshuffles which seeds work best, and a lucky training seed reaches the same FID with up to 2x less compute than an unlucky one. Based on these findings, we recommend a new FID evaluation protocol: evaluate under per-cell optimal guidance, treat any FID gap below the empirically measured ~1.3% CoV as inconclusive, and report an error bar over several training seeds rather than a single FID number.

</details>


### [129] [TimeProVe: Propose, then Verify for Efficient Long Video Temporal Reasoning in Activities of Daily Living](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20561)
*Arkaprava Sinha,Dominick Reilly,Siddharth Krishnan,Hieu Le,Srijan Das*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TimeProVe是一种成本高效的混合框架，通过轻量模块生成基于动作的证据假设，仅对关键部分调用昂贵VLM，在LVQA任务中性能提升7.3%，同时减少75%的VLM调用和93%的推理成本。同时提出开放基准OpenTSUBench用于评估日常活动场景中的时间定位推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长视频问答中，现有方法要么密集处理视频导致计算成本过高，要么依赖稀疏字幕推理而丢失时间定位和运动中心的证据，缺乏一种既能保持时间定位能力又具备成本效益的解决方案。

Method: 提出TimeProVe混合框架：首先使用轻量模块生成基于动作的答案-证据假设（ACE模块将时间定位动作转化为查询条件化的候选答案和支持证据窗口），然后仅对关键部分调用昂贵的VLM进行目标验证。

Result: 在OTB基准上比最强基线提升7.3%，VLM调用减少75%，推理成本降低93%；在Charades-STA上无需显式时间定位训练即取得竞争性能，结合定位VLM后达到SOTA。

Conclusion: TimeProVe证明了通过轻量级假设生成+定向VLM验证的混合策略，可以在保持时间定位推理能力的同时大幅降低计算成本，为长视频问答提供了一种实用的成本效益方案。

Abstract: Long Video Question Answering (LVQA) requires identifying sparse, query-relevant evidence within hours-long untrimmed videos. Existing approaches either process videos densely with large vision-language models (VLMs), incurring prohibitive computational cost, or rely on sparse caption-based reasoning, which often misses temporally localized and motion-centric evidence. We introduce TimeProVe, a cost-efficient hybrid framework for temporally grounded reasoning in long videos. TimeProVe first employs lightweight modules to generate action-grounded answer--evidence hypotheses and subsequently invokes an expensive VLM only for targeted verification. The core of our framework lies in the Action-based Candidate Evidence (ACE) module, which converts temporally localized actions into query-conditioned candidate answers and supporting evidence windows through lightweight LLM reasoning. We further introduce OpenTSUBench (OTB), an open-ended benchmark designed to evaluate temporally grounded reasoning in real-world Activities of Daily Living (ADL) scenarios. Experiments show that TimeProVe outperforms the strongest baseline on OTB by 7.3%, while reducing VLM calls by 75% and inference cost by 93%. Furthermore, without explicit temporal grounding training, TimeProVe achieves competitive performance on Charades-STA, and reaches state-of-the-art results when enhanced with grounding VLMs.

</details>


### [130] [JanusMesh: Fast and Zero-Shot 3D Visual Illusion Generation via Cross-Space Denoising](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20563)
*Siang-Ling Zhang,Huai-Hsun Cheng,Tsung-Ju Yang,Yu-Lun Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出JanusMesh，一个快速、无需训练的文本驱动3D视觉错觉生成框架。通过两阶段解耦：跨空间双分支去噪（CLIP对齐+SDF混合实现无缝几何融合）和视图条件纹理合成（投影聚合2D扩散先验），在3-5分钟内生成高质量双语义3D错觉，在几何完整性、语义可识别性和效率上显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D视觉错觉生成方法存在两大问题：(1) 基于优化的方法速度慢且产生过饱和颜色；(2) 朴素拼接方法无法产生几何一致的对象，导致可见的接缝和语义泄露。因此需要一种快速且高质量的生成框架。

Method: 两阶段解耦框架：第一阶段，提出跨空间双分支去噪过程，动态解码3D潜变量到体素空间，进行CLIP引导的方向对齐和符号距离场(SDF)混合，确保无缝几何融合；第二阶段，引入视图条件纹理合成模块，将特定视角的2D扩散先验投影并聚合到融合后的几何体上。

Result: 方法在3-5分钟内生成高度逼真的双语义3D错觉，在几何完整性、语义可识别性和效率方面显著优于现有方法。

Conclusion: 提出的JanusMesh框架通过解耦几何和纹理生成，实现了快速、高质量、无需训练的3D视觉错觉生成，有效解决了几何不一致和语义泄露问题。

Abstract: Creating 3D visual illusions, a single 3D mesh that reveals entirely different semantics from various viewing angles, is a fascinating but tough challenge. Existing optimization-based methods are slow and can produce oversaturated colors. In contrast, naive stitching approaches fail to produce geometrically coherent objects. This results in visible unnatural seams and semantic leaks. In this paper, we present a fast and training-free framework for generating text-driven 3D visual illusions. Our approach decouples the generation into two stages. First, we propose a cross-space dual-branch denoising process. This process dynamically decodes 3D latents into voxel space for CLIP-guided orientation alignment and Signed Distance Field (SDF) blending, which ensures seamless geometric fusion. Second, we introduce a view-conditioned texture synthesis module that projects and aggregates view-specific 2D diffusion priors onto the fused geometry. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our method generates highly realistic, dual-semantic 3D illusions in just 3-5 minutes. It significantly outperforms existing methods in geometric integrity, semantic recognizability, and efficiency. Project page: https://siang1105.github.io/JanusMesh.github.io/

</details>


### [131] [Semantic-Anchored Evidential Fusion for Domain-Robust Whole-Slide Survival Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19966)
*Yucheng Xing,Ling Huang,Pei Liu,Jingying Ma,Jiaqing Xu,Kai He,Mengling Feng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SAEFS框架，利用VQA提取语义锚点，结合主观逻辑融合证据，实现跨中心零样本生存预测，C-index提升10.2%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有WSI预后方法过度依赖像素级特征，在跨临床中心场景下泛化能力差，无法处理染色协议和扫描硬件引起的域偏移问题。

Method: 提出SAEFS框架：1) 通过VQA从WSI中提取语义锚点；2) 双流架构分别提取视觉和语义证据；3) 基于Dirichlet主观逻辑建模不确定性；4) 采用谨慎合取规则融合语义与视觉证据，避免过置信。

Result: 在单一源域训练、四个未见域零样本评估中，SAEFS在预测准确性和可靠性上均优于SOTA，平均C-index提升10.2%；VQA语义特征跨中心散度显著低于像素特征。

Conclusion: 高层病理语义（如肿瘤分级、微环境结构）可作为域不变表示，有效提升WSI预后模型在跨临床中心的泛化鲁棒性。

Abstract: Whole-slide images (WSIs) are widely used for computational cancer prognosis. However, most existing methods primarily focus on in-domain performance and fail to generalize across clinical centers. This limitation stems from their reliance on pixel-derived representations that are highly susceptible to domain-specific artifacts caused by staining protocols and scanner hardware. We hypothesize that high-level pathology semantics, such as tumor grade and micro-environmental architecture, provide a domain-invariant semantic representation that mirrors the robust diagnostic logic of human pathologists. Therefore, we propose a Semantic-Anchored Evidential Fusion Survival (SAEFS) framework, where SAEFS derives semantic anchors from WSIs via Visual Question Answering (VQA), employs a dual-stream WSI evidence extraction architecture, uses Dirichlet-based Subjective Logic to model uncertainty, and fuses semantic and visual evidence through a cautious conjunction rule to avoid overconfident fusion from correlated sources. Trained exclusively on one source domain and evaluated zero-shot across four unseen domains, SAEFS consistently outperforms state-of-the-art models both in prediction accuracy and reliability, improving the average C-index by 10.2%. Quantitative analyses further show that VQA-derived semantic features exhibit significantly lower cross-center divergence than pixel-derived features, highlighting their robustness for cross-center clinical applications.

</details>


### [132] [UNIEGO: Proxies as Mediators for Unified Egocentric Video Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20559)
*Wenhao Chi,Arkaprava Sinha,Dominick Reilly,Hieu Le,Srijan Das*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出UNIEGO，一个统一的第一人称视频编码器，通过层级化多教师蒸馏框架，融合多视角、多模态（RGB、深度、骨架）和四个基础模型的知识，利用代理模型（Proxy models）将异构教师知识转化为同质化表征，并通过选择性代理蒸馏（SPD）自适应筛选可靠监督信号，在三个第一人称视频理解任务上达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 第一人称视频理解受限于可穿戴摄像头的单一视角、单一模态和单一模型，无法充分捕捉人类动作的丰富性。需要一种能够整合跨视角、跨模态和跨基础模型互补知识，同时仅凭第一人称视频即可部署的统一表征。

Method: 提出层级化多教师蒸馏框架：1）引入表征特定的代理模型（Proxy models），将来自不同视角（ego/exo）、模态（RGB/深度/骨架）和四个基础模型的异构教师知识转化为同质化的第一人称空间；2）选择性代理蒸馏（SPD）：对每个训练样本自适应选择正确且置信度高的代理子集进行蒸馏，抑制错误信号；3）通过代理参数的可学习凸组合初始化UNIEGO，使模型在蒸馏前处于损失景观的良态区域。

Result: 在三个挑战性的第一人称-第三人称基准上，UNIEGO在动作识别、视频检索和动作分割三个任务上均达到SOTA性能，显著优于朴素的多教师蒸馏基线。

Conclusion: 结构化的、代理中介的知识迁移能够产生更丰富、更具判别力的第一人称表征，证明了通过层级化蒸馏整合异构知识源的有效性。

Abstract: Egocentric video understanding is inherently limited by the narrow perspective of wearable cameras: a single viewpoint, a single modality, a single model cannot capture the full richness of human action. We argue that a truly expressive egocentric representation must subsume complementary knowledge across viewpoints, modalities, and foundation model representations, yet remain deployable from egocentric video alone. To this end, we introduce a hierarchical multi-teacher distillation framework that produces UNIEGO, a unified egocentric encoder trained with nine teachers spanning ego-exo viewpoints, RGB, depth, and skeleton modalities, and four foundation models. Rather than distilling directly from heterogeneous teachers whose incompatible architectures and feature geometries induce conflicting gradients, our framework interposes a layer of representation-specific Proxy models that translate diverse teacher knowledge into a homogeneous egocentric space. A second distillation stage, Selective Proxy Distillation (SPD), then adaptively selects, for each training sample, the subset of proxies that are both correct and confident, distilling exclusively from reliable supervision and suppressing erroneous signals. SPD is further stabilized by initializing UNIEGO as a learned convex combination of proxy parameters, placing the unified model in a well-conditioned region of the loss landscape before distillation begins. UNIEGO achieves state-of-the-art performance across three egocentric video understanding tasks - action recognition, video retrieval, and action segmentation on three challenging ego-exo benchmarks, outperforming naive multi-teacher distillation baselines and demonstrating that structured, proxy-mediated knowledge transfer yields richer and more discriminative egocentric representations.

</details>


### [133] [ROSE: Benchmarking the Perception-to-Action Gap in Multimodal Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19965)
*Yihao Wang,Zijian He,Jie Ren,Keze Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出ROSE基准，通过固定视觉场景但改变区域约束和符号输出任务，测试多模态大模型在不同上下文中将相同视觉证据转换为所需动作的能力。实验发现，从计数任务到区域条件动作任务，模型性能最多下降44.5个百分点，揭示了模型在将共享视觉证据转化为上下文特定动作时存在独立的瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLLMs在处理固定视觉场景时，是否能在不同任务上下文中可靠地提取相同视觉证据并执行所需动作尚不清楚，缺乏系统性评估方法。

Method: 提出了ROSE（Reference-conditioned Oddity and Symbolic Execution）基准，通过耦合的计数和坐标动作任务，固定视觉场景同时变化区域约束和符号输出格式，评估模型推断隐式多数参考并基于细粒度视觉证据执行上下文相关动作的能力。

Result: 在9个最新MLLMs上的实验表明，从计数任务到区域条件动作任务，性能下降高达44.5个百分点（人类表现98.8%）；这种差距在相同模型能正确计数的配对场景和区域上依然存在；全局点击和匹配局部控制实验显示，坐标定位仅能解释部分性能损失。

Conclusion: MLLMs在处理不同任务上下文时，存在一个独立于坐标定位能力之外的、模型特有的瓶颈，即难以将共享的视觉证据可靠地转化为上下文特定的动作。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) are increasingly expected to act on visual information, yet the same scene may require different actions under different task contexts. How reliably can a model turn the same visual evidence into the action required by the current context? To answer this question, we introduce \textsc{ROSE} (\textbf{R}eference-conditioned \textbf{O}ddity and \textbf{S}ymbolic \textbf{E}xecution), a controlled benchmark that holds the visual scene fixed while varying region constraints and required symbolic outputs. Through coupled counting and coordinate-action tasks, \textsc{ROSE} tests whether models can infer an implicit majority reference and act on the resulting fine-grained visual evidence under changing contexts. Across nine recent MLLMs, performance drops by as much as 44.5 percentage points from counting-oriented tasks to region-conditioned action, despite 98.8\% human performance. The gap persists on paired scenes and regions for which the same model returns the correct count, while global-click and matched local controls show that coordinate grounding explains only part of the loss, revealing a distinct, model-dependent bottleneck in turning shared visual evidence into context-specific actions.

</details>


<div id='cs.CR'></div>

# cs.CR [[Back]](#toc)

### [134] [Secure Coding Drift in LLM-Assisted Post-Quantum Cryptography Development: A Gamified Fix](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19474)
*R. D. N. Shakya,C. P. Wijesiriwardana,S. M. Vidanagamachchi,Nalin A. G. Arachchilage*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出“后量子密码学中的安全编码漂移”这一社会技术漏洞模型，描述因持续依赖LLM生成代码导致安全编码实践逐渐退化，并提出一种游戏化、LLM增强的安全编码框架，将LLM从被动助手转变为主动安全副驾驶。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 向后量子密码学（PQC）的过渡存在显著的实现复杂性（常量时间执行、侧信道抵抗、精确参数化），而LLM虽提升开发效率但常生成不安全或次优代码。现有工作仅关注静态漏洞，缺乏对长期人机交互中安全实践退化这一动态过程的研究。

Method: 提出Secure Coding Drift in PQC模型，将安全风险概念化为纵向行为现象；设计游戏化、LLM增强的安全编码框架，集成对抗性评估、行为反馈和安全评分机制，嵌入开发工作流。

Result: 将LLM从被动辅助工具重塑为主动安全副驾驶，为实现更安全的PQC实现提供了AI中介环境下的方法论框架。

Conclusion: 持续依赖LLM生成代码会导致安全编码实践逐渐退化（安全编码漂移），需要引入对抗性评估和行为反馈机制来缓解这一风险；游戏化LLM增强框架可有效提升PQC实现的安全性。

Abstract: The transition to Post Quantum Cryptography (PQC) introduces considerable implementation complexity, requiring strict adherence to constant-time execution, side channel resistance, and precise parametrisation. Simultaneously, large language models (LLMs) are heavily embedded in software development workflows, including cryptographic engineering. While LLMs improve productivity, evidence shows that they frequently generate insecure or suboptimal code, particularly in security critical domains. This paper introduces Secure Coding Drift in PQC, a novel socio technical vulnerability model capturing the gradual degradation of secure coding practices due to sustained reliance on LLM-generated code. Unlike prior work that focuses on static vulnerabilities, we conceptualise security risk as a longitudinal behavioural phenomenon rising from human AI interaction. To mitigate this, we propose a gamified, LLM augmented secure coding framework that embeds adversarial evaluation, behavioural feedback, and security scoring into development workflows. Our approach reframes LLMs from passive assistants into active security co-pilots, contributing toward safer PQC implementation in AI mediated environments.

</details>


### [135] [FloatDoor: Platform-Triggered Backdoors in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19535)
*Nils Loose,Jonas Sander,Felix Mächtle,Thomas Eisenbarth*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出FloatDoor，首个输入无关、由平台触发的针对生成式LLM的后门攻击。通过两个轻量LoRA适配器，攻击模型在特定目标平台上表现出恶意行为（如生成含漏洞代码），在其他平台则表现正常。攻击利用模型审计与部署之间的TOCTOU间隙，在多种硬件平台上验证有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在不同部署平台上因浮点运算非结合性和内核实现差异会产生输出不一致，这种平台依赖的可变性此前未被从安全攻击角度充分研究。作者旨在揭示这一现象带来的新型攻击面。

Method: FloatDoor包含两个LoRA适配器：第一个放大跨平台数值发散以产生可区分的平台签名，第二个将该签名绑定到恶意下游任务（如代码漏洞注入）。攻击是输入无关的，即任何输入在目标平台上都会触发恶意行为。利用模型审计（检查）与部署（使用）之间的时间差。

Result: 在Qwen3-4B上对NVIDIA GPU、Google TPU、AWS Graviton、阿里Yitian-710等多种平台进行了验证。案例研究表明FloatDoor能在目标平台上可靠地诱导出可利用的代码漏洞，同时保持模型在其他平台上的正常效用。

Conclusion: 本文建立了针对LLM部署的新一类攻击，强调了在敏感LLM应用中建立可信模型供应链的迫切需求。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed in sensitive settings such as software engineering, where their outputs directly shape downstream artifacts. Recent work has shown that an identical model can produce measurably different outputs depending on the deployment platform, a consequence of non-associative floating-point arithmetic and divergent kernel implementations. We study the security implications of this platform-dependent variability and uncover a novel attack surface on LLM deployments. We introduce FloatDoor, the first input-independent, platform-triggered backdoor attack against generative LLMs. The compromised model exhibits adversary-chosen behavior when served on a target platform and is otherwise benign. FloatDoor is realized through two lightweight LoRA adapters, one that amplifies inter-platform numerical divergence and one that binds the resulting platform signature to a malicious downstream task, while leaving aggregate model utility largely intact. FloatDoor exploits a pronounced time-of-check, time-of-use gap between model auditing and serving. We demonstrate FloatDoor on Qwen3-4B across a broad range of deployment targets, including NVIDIA GPUs, Google TPUs, AWS Graviton, and Alibaba Yitian-710. As a final case study, we show that FloatDoor reliably induces exploitable code vulnerabilities on a chosen target platform. Our results establish a new class of attacks on LLM deployments and underscore the pressing need for trusted model supply chains in sensitive, LLM-powered applications.

</details>


### [136] [G-Lox: Group-Adaptive, Privacy-Preserving Bridge Distribution with Two-Party Computation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19620)
*Baigang Chen,Nicholas Hopper*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: G-Lox是一个保持Lox风格分配器盲性的桥接分发系统，通过双服务器隐私墙实现隐藏的、有状态的群组级自适应分配，采用DPF/FSS和两方安全计算协议保护隐私状态访问，在C++/EMP实现中展现出低客户端开销（M=1024时仅约0.25秒/轮次），模拟实验表明其在群组特定阻断和Sybil枚举场景下优于Lox和rBridge等基线系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有桥接分发系统如Lox虽然提供了分配器盲性保护，但缺乏群组级自适应能力。作者希望在不牺牲分配器盲性的前提下，实现有状态的群组级自适应分配，以提升系统面对群组特定阻断和Sybil攻击时的鲁棒性。

Method: 采用双服务器隐私墙架构，将自适应分配逻辑置于两服务器之间，确保单一服务器无法获知群组标识符或群组到桥接的分配信息。利用两方DPF/FSS（分布式点函数/函数秘密共享）协议和安全两方计算实现私有状态访问与状态依赖更新，支持阻断报告、传输感知重分配和隐私保护群组分拆。

Result: 在真实TCP套接字上的C++/EMP实现中，私有状态访问的客户端可见开销很低：状态规模达2^16时，每轮次通信量保持在低KiB范围。在M=1024配置下，客户端发送1,968字节、接收1,280字节，每轮迭代约需0.25秒。模拟实验表明，在群组特定阻断和Sybil枚举场景下，G-Lox相较于Lox和rBridge等基线系统在保持广泛分发能力的同时显著提升了鲁棒性。

Conclusion: G-Lox成功在保持Lox风格分配器盲性的前提下，实现了隐藏且有状态的群组级自适应分配，并通过安全协议设计确保了隐私保护，系统开销可接受，鲁棒性优于现有基线方案。

Abstract: We present G-Lox (group-adaptive Lox), a bridge-distribution system that preserves Lox-style distributor blindness while enabling hidden, stateful group-level adaptation. G-Lox places adaptive assignment logic behind a two-server privacy wall, so no single server learns group identifiers or group-to-bridge assignments. Private state access and state-dependent updates use two-server DPF/FSS protocols and secure two-party computation, supporting blockage reporting, transport-aware reassignment, and privacy-preserving group splitting.
  We evaluate G-Lox through system measurements and policy simulation. In our C++/EMP implementation over real TCP sockets, private state access has low client-visible overhead: across state sizes up to 2^16, communication remains in the low-KiB range per iteration. At M=1024, the client sends 1,968 bytes, receives 1,280 bytes, and completes an iteration in about 0.25 s. Simulations with group-specific blocking and Sybil enumeration show that G-Lox improves robustness over Lox- and rBridge-like baselines among systems that maintain broad issuance.

</details>


### [137] [PUFFERDOS: Efficient and Effective Attack String Generation for Regular Expression Denial of Service Vulnerabilities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19654)
*Shangzhi Xu,Ziqi Ding,Xiao Cheng,Yuekang Li,Nan Sun,Benjamin Turnbull,Shuangxiang Kan,Siqi Ma*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了PUFFERDOS，一种能够生成实际可利用的ReDoS攻击字符串的工具，它通过定义三种漏洞模式、综合生成攻击字符串，并利用特定于ReDoS的组合符号执行进行验证，解决了现有攻击字符串不切实际（长度预算过大、未在程序级验证）的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有ReDoS攻击字符串生成技术（静态分析和动态探索）产生的攻击字符串往往不切实际，因为它们假设不现实的输入长度预算，且未在程序级验证有效性和效率，导致许多字符串在实际程序中无法触发漏洞，限制了实际可用性。

Method: 首先基于观察和形式化验证定义三种易受攻击的模式；然后根据这些模式进行综合技术生成攻击字符串；最后通过特定于ReDoS的组合符号执行对字符串进行精炼和验证，确保在实际程序中的可利用性。

Result: PUFFERDOS能够生成在现实长度预算内可行且在程序级经过验证的攻击输入，有效利用真实世界程序中的ReDoS漏洞。

Conclusion: 通过定义漏洞模式、综合生成和组合符号执行验证，PUFFERDOS显著提高了ReDoS攻击字符串的实际可用性，解决了现有方法在实用性和有效性方面的关键不足。

Abstract: ReDoS attacks constitute a critical class of resource-exhaustion vulnerabilities. In such attacks, adversaries exploit the pathological worst-case execution behavior of regular expression (regex) engines to induce highly asymmetric computational workloads, ultimately exhausting system resources and degrading service availability. To protect systems against ReDoS attacks, numerous detection techniques have been proposed that simulate the attack process by generating attack strings to proactively exploit ReDoS vulnerabilities at the early development stage and facilitate remediation. Existing techniques broadly fall into two classes: static analyses that search for pathological regex structures, and dynamic exploration methods that synthesize candidate attack strings. However, the generated attack strings are often impractical for real-world exploitation because they usually assume unrealistic input-length budgets and do not validate the effectiveness and efficiency of the attack at the program level. Therefore, many generated strings fail to trigger vulnerable regexes when applied to real-world programs, further limiting the practical utility. To address these shortcomings, we introduce an effective and efficient attack string generator, PUFFERDOS, designed to synthesize attack inputs that are both feasible within realistic length budgets and validated at the program level, enabling effective exploitation of ReDoS vulnerabilities in real-world programs. Specifically, we first define three vulnerable patterns based on our observation and formal verification. According to the patterns, PUFFERDOS conducts a synthesis technique to generate attack strings, and then refines and validates the strings with ReDoS-specific compositional concolic execution to guarantee real-world exploitability.

</details>


### [138] [A Layered Security Framework Against Prompt Injection in RAG-Based Chatbots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19660)
*Gulshan Saleem,Nisar Ahmed,Muhammad Imran Zaman,Ali Hassan*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出了一种三层的提示注入防御框架，涵盖输入过滤、上下文组装和输出审计三个环节，能有效降低LLM应用中的直接与间接提示注入攻击成功率，从71.4%降至11.3%，误报率4.8%，延迟开销61.2ms。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有防御手段仅在单一流水线阶段运作，无法同时防御直接和间接提示注入攻击，尤其是RAG系统中通过中毒知识库文档发起的间接注入攻击。

Method: 三层防御框架：Layer 1基于规则模式库和微调语义异常分类器筛查用户输入；Layer 2在上下文组装阶段实施基于来源的指令层级，防止检索内容覆盖操作策略；Layer 3通过策略规则引擎和语义漂移检测器审计模型输出，并引入持续审计循环支持分类器重训练。

Result: 在GPT-4o、Llama 3、Mistral 7B上评测5080个样本，攻击成功率从71.4%降至11.3%，比最佳单层基线高27.3个百分点，比已有关卡系统高23.8个百分点，误报率4.8%，中位延迟61.2ms。消融实验证实三层保护互补，联合效果超过个体贡献之和。

Conclusion: 该框架是模型无关的中间件方案，能在不修改底层LLM的前提下显著降低提示注入攻击风险，且具有持续适应新攻击模式的能力。

Abstract: Prompt injection is ranked as the most critical vulnerability in large language model (LLM) deployments by the OWASP Top 10 for LLM Applications, yet existing defenses operate at isolated pipeline stages and remain incomplete. Input filters cannot inspect retrieved documents, while output monitors cannot prevent malicious payloads from reaching the model. Consequently, retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) chatbots remain vulnerable to indirect injection, where a poisoned knowledge-base document compromises every user whose query retrieves it. We present a three-layer framework that intercepts both direct and indirect prompt injection throughout the inference pipeline. Layer 1 screens user input using a rule-based pattern library and a fine-tuned semantic anomaly classifier. Layer 2 enforces a provenance-based instruction hierarchy during context assembly, preventing retrieved content from overriding operator policy. Layer 3 audits model output using a policy rule engine and semantic drift detector before delivery. A continuous audit loop aggregates structured logs and supports retraining to adapt the classifier to emerging attack patterns. The framework is model-agnostic and deploys as middleware without modifying the underlying LLM. Evaluation on 5,080 samples across GPT-4o, Llama 3, and Mistral 7B shows that the framework reduces Attack Success Rate (ASR) from 71.4\% to 11.3\%, outperforming the best single-layer baseline by 27.3 percentage points and a published guardrail system by 23.8 percentage points, while maintaining a 4.8\% false positive rate and a median latency overhead of 61.2 ms. Ablation studies confirm that all three layers provide complementary protection and that their combined effect exceeds the sum of individual contributions.

</details>


### [139] [When Global Gating Is Enough: Admission-Time Hubness Control in Anisotropic Vector Retrieval Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19692)
*Prashant Kumar Pathak,Tarun Kumar Sharma*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种RAG系统中针对向量集线性（hubness）投毒攻击的准入控制方法，通过哨兵查询评分在文档插入前隔离类似集线器的文档，实现无暴露窗口的防御。全局门控在10万文档规模上达到召回率1.0（有效范围≥0.92），HotFlip攻击下0.91±0.07，假阳性率1%，插入开销与语料库规模无关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RAG系统中向量集线性现象导致少数点成为大量查询的最近邻，存在单文档投毒可影响无关请求的安全风险。现有基于周期性反向kNN扫描的防御存在暴露窗口和重复全语料库计算开销问题。

Method: 提出准入时控制方法：在文档插入前，使用哨兵查询集对候选文档进行评分，识别并隔离类似集线器的文档。设计了全局门控和每主题门控两种变体，阈值通过增量方式维护。在HNSW索引上评估了近似索引对决策的影响。

Result: 在两个10万文档语料库、五种编码器、不相交的攻击者和防御者查询集上：全局门控在关键嵌入空间点召回率1.0（有效范围≥0.92），HotFlip攻击下0.91±0.07，一般文档假阳性率1%。每主题门控无可靠收益。准入控制在HNSW上增加约3.1%的摄入延迟，评分在10^6向量规模下保持稳定，近似索引下仅1.2%的决策翻转且不涉及攻击。

Conclusion: 准入控制可有效防御RAG中的集线性投毒攻击，全局门控优于每主题门控。方法具有与语料库规模无关的插入成本和平摊删除成本。对于自然或紧密域集线器，需结合来源追踪作为补充。

Abstract: Vector hubness, where a few points become nearest neighbors of many queries, creates a poisoning risk in retrieval-augmented generation (RAG): one injected document can influence unrelated requests. Existing defenses use periodic reverse-kNN scans, leaving an exposure window and repeated corpus-wide work. We study admission-time control, scoring each candidate against sentinel queries and quarantining hub-like documents before insertion. Across two 100,000-document corpora, five encoders, and disjoint attacker and defender query sets, a global gate achieves recall 1.0 at the decisive embedding-space point (>=0.92 across the effective range) and 0.91 +/- 0.07 on HotFlip attacks, with 1% false positives on general documents. A per-topic gate provides no reliable benefit, consistent with anisotropy coupling local and global visibility. Thresholds are maintained incrementally, with corpus-size-independent insertion cost and amortized deletion cost. On HNSW, admission adds about 3.1% to ingestion latency, scoring remains flat to 10^6 vectors, and 1.2% of decisions flip under approximate indexing, none involving attacks. Provenance complements the gate for natural or tight-domain hubs.

</details>


### [140] [SafeSpec: Fast and Safe LLM via Dynamic Reflective Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19755)
*Haotian Xu,Zeyang Zhang,Linbao Li,Huadi Zheng,Yu Li,Cheng Zhuo*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: SafeSpec提出了一种安全感知的推测性推理框架，通过在验证过程中集成风险估计，在保持推测解码加速优势的同时提升LLM的安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有安全防御机制与推测性解码存在根本性不兼容：要么引入额外计算开销，要么破坏草稿-验证机制，导致加速收益丧失。亟需一种能够同时兼顾推测加速和推理时安全的方法。

Method: 在目标模型上附加一个轻量级潜在安全头，在单次前向传播中联合评估语义有效性和安全性。检测到不安全生成时，采用回滚和安全引导的反射式多重采样恢复安全延续，而非终止生成。将越狱攻击建模为生成轨迹上的分布偏移，进行风险感知的轨迹恢复。

Result: 在多个模型和对抗性基准上，SafeSpec显著提升了安全-效率权衡。在Qwen3-32B上，攻击成功率降低15%，同时在良性负载上保持2.06倍的推理加速。

Conclusion: 推测性加速和推理时安全性可以联合优化，二者并非互斥目标。

Abstract: Speculative inference accelerates large language model (LLM) decoding but provides no inherent safety guarantees. Existing safety defenses are largely incompatible with speculative inference: they either introduce additional computation or disrupt the draft-verify mechanism, negating acceleration benefits. This reveals a fundamental incompatibility between current safety methods and speculative decoding. We propose SafeSpec, a safety-aware speculative inference framework that integrates risk estimation directly into the verification process. SafeSpec attaches a lightweight latent safety head to the target model to jointly evaluate semantic validity and safety in a single forward pass. When unsafe generations are detected, SafeSpec applies rollback and safety-guided reflective multi-sampling to recover safe continuations rather than terminating generation. We model jailbreak attacks as distributional shifts over generative trajectories, where adversarial prompts increase the probability of harmful continuations without eliminating safe ones. Under this model, SafeSpec performs risk-aware trajectory recovery within the speculative decoding process. Across multiple models and adversarial benchmarks, SafeSpec achieves a substantially improved safety-efficiency trade-off. On Qwen3-32B, SafeSpec reduces attack success rates by 15% while preserving a 2.06x inference speedup on benign workloads, demonstrating that speculative acceleration and inference-time safety can be jointly optimized.

</details>


### [141] [DISARM: Target Electronic Device Informed Mitigation of Software Runtime Side-Channel Vulnerabilities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19807)
*Tasneem Suha,Tanzim Mahfuz,Rima Asmar Awad,Prabuddha Chakraborty*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: DISARM是一种联合硬件-软件方法，通过利用真实嵌入式设备的时序值生成针对性软件补丁，来缓解运行时侧信道漏洞，在22个基准测试上优于现有方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有运行时侧信道攻击缓解技术未考虑底层硬件或设备的时序特性，导致过度修复、修复不足甚至失败的问题。

Method: 提出DISARM，一种联合硬件-软件方法论，利用真实嵌入式设备的时序值来生成针对性的软件修复，支持C、C++和Java源代码。

Result: 在22个标准基准测试和5种不同嵌入式/边缘设备上，DISARM在执行时间开销、代码大小开销和正确性方面均优于PENDULUM和DifFuzzAR等最先进方案。

Conclusion: 考虑底层硬件时序特性的联合硬件-软件方法能更有效地缓解运行时侧信道漏洞，避免了不考虑硬件的传统方法所存在的问题。

Abstract: Program runtime or timing attacks exploit variations in a program's execution times to extract sensitive information from the program (e.g. encryption keys, sensitive variable data, intellectual property). State-of-the-art solutions to runtime side-channel attacks attempt to balance the execution time of the sensitive code for different control flow paths to eliminate the timing leakage. However, during the mitigation process, most techniques do not consider the underlying hardware or device on which the target program is supposed to run on. This can lead to over-fixing (unnecessary extra operations), under-fixing (not solving the imbalance properly), and even failures. We propose DISARM, a joint hardware-software methodology (unlike any existing solution) for mitigating runtime side-channel vulnerabilities that utilizes timing values from real embedded devices to generate targeted software fixes. We implement DISARM to support C, C++, and Java source codes and validate it across 22 standard benchmarks. DISARM outperforms state-of-the-art solutions such as PENDULUM and DifFuzzAR in terms of execution time overhead, code size overhead, and correctness on five different embedded or edge devices.

</details>


### [142] [Heterogeneous LLM Debate Under Adversarial Peers: Honest Gains, Replacement Costs, and Resilience](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19826)
*Prashanti Nilayam,Kiran Kumar Ramanna,Prashil Tumbade,Sankalp Nayak*
